Acts once amount_refunded covers the credit portion\n463\t *   (charge total minus the non-refundable fee).\n464\t *\n465\t * TRACE PATH:\n466\t * Charge -> PaymentIntent -> Checkout Session -> license_keys.stripe_session_id\n467\t *\n468\t * IDEMPOTENCY:\n469\t * License revocation is idempotent (\"revoked\" status). Credit deduction is\n470\t * recorded in stripe_processed_events keyed by charge.id, so retried\n471\t * charge.refunded events never double-deduct.\n472\t */\n473\texport async function handleChargeRefunded(\n474\t  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n475\t  eventId: string\n476\t): Promise<void> {\n477\t  console.log(\n478\t    `Processing refund for charge ${charge.id} (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount})`\n479\t  );\n480\t\n481\t  // STEP 2: Get PaymentIntent ID from the charge\n482\t  const paymentIntentId =\n483\t    typeof charge.payment_intent === \"string\"\n484\t      ? charge.payment_intent\n485\t      : charge.payment_intent?.id;\n486\t\n487\t  if (!paymentIntentId) {\n488\t    console.log(\n489\t      \"No payment_intent on charge, skipping (likely not a Checkout purchase)\"\n490\t    );\n491\t    return;\n492\t  }\n493\t\n494\t  // STEP 3: Find the Checkout Session associated with this PaymentIntent\n495\t  const sessions = await stripe.checkout.sessions.list({\n496\t    payment_intent: paymentIntentId,\n497\t    limit: 1,\n498\t  });\n499\t\n500\t  if (sessions.data.length === 0) {\n501\t    console.log(\n502\t      `No checkout session found for payment_intent ${paymentIntentId}`\n503\t    );\n504\t    return;\n505\t  }\n506\t\n507\t  const checkoutSession = sessions.data[0];\n508\t  const purchaseType = checkoutSession.metadata?.purchase_type;\n509\t\n510\t  // STEP 4: Route by purchase type, applying the right \"is this refund\n511\t  // actionable?\" rule for each.\n512\t  if (purchaseType === \"credits\") {\n513\t    // The 6% fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a credit refund\n514\t    // refunds only the credit value — a partial Stripe refund. Act once the\n515\t    // refunded amount covers the credit portion (charge total minus the fee).\n516\t    const feeCents = parseInt(checkoutSession.metadata?.fee_cents || \"0\", 10);\n517\t    const creditPortion = charge.amount - feeCents;\n518\t    if (charge.amount_refunded < creditPortion) {\n519\t      console.log(\n520\t        `Refund ${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount} does not cover the credit portion (${creditPortion}) for credits session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n521\t      );\n522\t      return;\n523\t    }\n524\t    await handleCreditRefund(charge, checkoutSession, eventId);\n525\t    return;\n526\t  }\n527\t\n528\t  if (purchaseType !== \"license\") {\n529\t    console.log(\n530\t      `Refund for unknown purchase type (${purchaseType}), skipping`\n531\t    );\n532\t    return;\n533\t  }\n534\t\n535\t  // License purchases: act only on a FULL refund.\n536\t  if (charge.amount_refunded !== charge.amount) {\n537\t    console.log(\n538\t      `Partial refund (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount}) for license session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n539\t    );\n540\t    return;\n541\t  }\n542\t\n543\t  console.log(`Revoking license for checkout session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n544\t\n545\t  // STEP 5: Revoke the license in database\n546\t  const license = await findAccountByStripeSession(checkoutSession.id);\n547\t\n548\t  if (!license) {\n549\t    console.error(`License not found for session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n550\t    return;\n551\t  }\n552\t\n553\t  await refundCreditGrant({\n554\t    sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n555\t    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n556\t  });\n557\t\n558\t  // Already revoked - idempotent, just log and return\n559\t  if (license.status === \"revoked\") {\n560\t    console.log(`License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... already revoked`);\n561\t    return;\n562\t  }\n563\t\n564\t  // STEP 6: Update status to revoked\n565\t  await updateAccountKey(license.id, { status: \"revoked\" });\n566\t\n567\t  console.log(\n568\t    `License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... revoked due to full refund`\n569\t  );\n570\t}\n571\t\n572\t/**\n573\t * Reverse a refunded credit-pack purchase.\n574\t *\n575\t * Looks up the original credit grant from the checkout session metadata and\n576\t * atomically deducts it (clamped at 0). Idempotent via stripe_processed_events\n577\t * keyed by charge.id, so webhook retries never double-deduct.\n578\t */\n579\tasync function handleCreditRefund(\n580\t  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n581\t  checkoutSession: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n582\t  eventId: string\n583\t): Promise<void> {\n584\t  // license_key is only present on top-ups; minted credit purchases carry none.\n585\t  // The clawback is keyed on the checkout session id (the grant's source_id),\n586\t  // so it works for both paths — license_key is informational only here.\n587\t  const licenseKey = checkoutSession.metadata?.license_key;\n588\t  const creditAmount = parseInt(\n589\t    checkoutSession.metadata?.credit_amount || \"0\",\n590\t    10\n591\t  );\n592\t\n593\t  if (!creditAmount || creditAmount <= 0) {\n594\t    console.error(\n595\t      `Credit refund for session ${checkoutSession.id}: invalid metadata (credit_amount=${checkoutSession.metadata?.credit_amount}), skipping`\n596\t    );\n597\t    return;\n598\t  }\n599\t\n600\t  const result = await refundCreditGrant({\n601\t    sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n602\t    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n603\t  });\n604\t\n605\t  if (result.status === \"duplicate\") {\n606\t    console.log(\n607\t      `Credit refund already processed for charge ${charge.id}, skipping`\n608\t    );\n609\t    return;\n610\t  }\n611\t\n612\t  const licenseHint = licenseKey ? ` (license ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...)` : \"\";\n613\t  console.log(\n614\t    `Deducted ${result.refundedAmount} credits for refunded charge ${charge.id}${licenseHint}`\n615\t  );\n616\t}\n617\t"}]},"uuid":"393e62b5-3bc0-4a53-8ae5-0141d2105c49","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:40:19.484Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/lib/services/stripe-webhook.ts","content":"import Stripe from \"stripe\";\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport { emailService } from \"@/lib/services/email\";\nimport { generateLicenseKey } from \"@/lib/services/license-key\";\nimport {\n  findAccountByKey,\n  getAccountKeysByEmail,\n  findAccountByStripeSession,\n  insertAccountKey,\n  getOrCreateUser,\n  updateAccountKey,\n  grantCreditLot,\n  grantCreditsForStripeEvent,\n  refundCreditGrant,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Webhook Handlers\n *\n * Service module for processing Stripe webhook events.\n * Handles license purchases and credit purchases.\n */\n\n/**\n * Process a completed license purchase.\n *\n * CRITICAL: This function must be idempotent.\n * Stripe may send the same event multiple times.\n * We use upsert with stripe_session_id as the idempotency key.\n */\nexport async function handleLicensePurchase(\n  session: Stripe.Checkout.Session\n): Promise<void> {\n  const customerEmail = session.customer_details?.email;\n  const customerName =\n    session.customer_details?.name ||\n    customerEmail?.split(\"@\")[0] ||\n    \"Customer\";\n  const stripeCustomerId = session.customer as string;\n\n  if (!customerEmail) {\n    throw new Error(\"No customer email in checkout session\");\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Processing license purchase for ${customerEmail}`);\n\n  // STEP 1: Check if we already processed this session (idempotency)\n  const existingLicense = await findAccountByStripeSession(session.id);\n\n  if (existingLicense) {\n    console.log(\n      `License already exists for session ${session.id}, resending email...`\n    );\n    // Still send email in case it failed before\n    await sendLicenseEmail(customerName, customerEmail, existingLicense.key);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 2: Generate unique license key with collision check\n  let licenseKey: string;\n  let attempts = 0;\n  const maxAttempts = 10;\n\n  do {\n    licenseKey = generateLicenseKey();\n    attempts++;\n\n    // Check uniqueness in database\n    const collision = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n    if (!collision) break;\n\n    console.warn(`License key collision detected, retrying... (${attempts})`);\n\n    if (attempts >= maxAttempts) {\n      throw new Error(\"Failed to generate unique license key after max attempts\");\n    }\n  } while (true);\n\n  console.log(`Generated license key: ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...`);\n\n  // STEP 3: Get or create user for the customer\n  const user = await getOrCreateUser(customerEmail, {\n    name: customerName,\n    stripeCustomerId,\n  });\n\n  if (!user) {\n    throw new Error(`Failed to create user for ${customerEmail}`);\n  }\n\n  console.log(`User ready for ${customerEmail}: ${user.id}`);\n\n  // STEP 4: Store license in database\n  let insertedLicense;\n  try {\n    insertedLicense = await insertAccountKey({\n      key: licenseKey,\n      email: customerEmail.toLowerCase().trim(),\n      userId: user.id,\n      stripeCustomerId,\n      stripeSessionId: session.id,\n      status: \"granted\",\n    });\n  } catch (insertError: unknown) {\n    // Check if it's a duplicate (race condition with webhook retry)\n    if (\n      insertError &&\n      typeof insertError === \"object\" &&\n      \"code\" in insertError &&\n      (insertError as { code: string }).code === \"23505\"\n    ) {\n      console.log(\"License already inserted by concurrent request\");\n      return;\n    }\n    console.error(\"Failed to store license key:\", insertError);\n    throw insertError;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`License key stored in database for ${customerEmail}`);\n\n  // STEP 4b: Grant initial credits\n  if (insertedLicense) {\n    try {\n      await grantCreditLot({\n        userId: insertedLicense.userId,\n        amount: 5000,\n        sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n        sourceId: session.id,\n      });\n      console.log(`Granted 5000 initial credits for license ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...`);\n    } catch (creditError) {\n      console.error(\"Failed to create initial credit balance:\", creditError);\n      // Don't throw - license was created, credits can be added later\n    }\n  }\n\n  // STEP 5: Send license email\n  await sendLicenseEmail(customerName, customerEmail, licenseKey);\n}\n\n/**\n * Send the license key email to the customer.\n *\n * Uses the existing email service with retry logic.\n * Does not throw - logs errors but allows the webhook to succeed.\n */\nasync function sendLicenseEmail(\n  customerName: string,\n  customerEmail: string,\n  licenseKey: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  const emailResult = await emailService.sendLicenseKey({\n    customerName,\n    customerEmail,\n    licenseKey,\n    productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n    downloadUrl: \"https://www.hyperwhisper.com\",\n    supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n  });\n\n  if (!emailResult.success) {\n    // Log but don't throw - license is created, email can be resent manually\n    console.error(`Failed to send license email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n  } else {\n    console.log(`License email sent to ${customerEmail}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Process a completed credit purchase.\n *\n * Two paths, decided by whether the checkout carried a license key:\n * - TOP-UP (`metadata.license_key` present): add credits to the existing key.\n * - MINT (`metadata.license_key` absent): a guest bought credits with no key,\n *   so we mint one, grant the credits, and email the new key. This is now the\n *   only way to obtain a key (the standalone license product is retired).\n *\n * CRITICAL: idempotent. Stripe may deliver the same event multiple times.\n * Mint dedupes on license_keys.stripe_session_id; the grant dedupes on\n * stripe_processed_events(stripe_object_id = session.id).\n */\nexport async function handleCreditPurchase(\n  session: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n  eventId: string,\n  eventType = \"checkout.session.completed\"\n): Promise<void> {\n  const licenseKey = session.metadata?.license_key;\n  const creditAmount = parseInt(session.metadata?.credit_amount || \"0\", 10);\n\n  if (!creditAmount || creditAmount <= 0) {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Invalid credit amount in metadata: ${session.metadata?.credit_amount}`\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (session.payment_status !== \"paid\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Skipping credit purchase for session ${session.id}: payment_status=${session.payment_status}`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (licenseKey) {\n    await handleCreditTopUp(session, eventId, eventType, licenseKey, creditAmount);\n  } else {\n    await handleCreditMint(session, eventId, eventType, creditAmount);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Top-up path: grant credits to an existing license key and email a receipt.\n */\nasync function handleCreditTopUp(\n  session: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n  eventId: string,\n  eventType: string,\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditAmount: number\n): Promise<void> {\n  console.log(\n    `Processing credit top-up: ${creditAmount} credits for license ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...`\n  );\n\n  const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n  if (!license) {\n    throw new Error(`License not found: ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...`);\n  }\n\n  // Refuse to credit a non-granted (e.g. revoked) license: those credits would be\n  // unusable via /api/license/credits. Throw so the webhook returns non-2xx and the\n  // event surfaces for manual review/refund instead of silently crediting a dead row.\n  if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n    throw new Error(\n      `Cannot grant credits to ${license.status} license: ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...`\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Idempotently record the checkout session and atomically grant credits.\n  const grantResult = await grantCreditsForStripeEvent({\n    eventId,\n    eventType,\n    stripeObjectId: session.id,\n    userId: license.userId,\n    creditAmount,\n    // Explicit (matches the mint path); the refund clawback resolves grants by\n    // (sourceType, sourceId), so keep both paths writing the same provenance.\n    sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n    sourceId: session.id,\n  });\n\n  if (grantResult === \"duplicate\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Credit top-up already processed for session ${session.id}, skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Credits added: ${creditAmount} for session ${session.id}`);\n\n  // Email a receipt with the amount added and the new balance.\n  const newBalance = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n  const customerEmail = license.email;\n  const customerName =\n    session.customer_details?.name || customerEmail?.split(\"@\")[0] || \"Customer\";\n\n  const emailResult = await emailService.sendCreditTopUp({\n    customerName,\n    customerEmail,\n    licenseKey,\n    creditAmount,\n    newBalance,\n    productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n    supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n  });\n\n  if (!emailResult.success) {\n    console.error(`Failed to send top-up email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Mint path: a guest bought credits with no key. Generate a key, grant the\n * credits onto it, and email the key + starting balance.\n *\n * Idempotency: license insert is guarded by the unique stripe_session_id index\n * (we look it up first, and catch a concurrent insert's 23505); the credit\n * grant is independently idempotent on session.id, so it is safe to (re)run on\n * every delivery — retries never double-grant and always converge to a key with\n * the credits attached.\n */\nasync function handleCreditMint(\n  session: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n  eventId: string,\n  eventType: string,\n  creditAmount: number\n): Promise<void> {\n  const customerEmail = session.customer_details?.email;\n  const customerName =\n    session.customer_details?.name || customerEmail?.split(\"@\")[0] || \"Customer\";\n  const stripeCustomerId = (session.customer as string) || null;\n\n  if (!customerEmail) {\n    throw new Error(\"No customer email in credit checkout session\");\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Processing credit purchase by ${customerEmail}`);\n\n  // STEP 1: Resolve the license for this session (existing on retry, else mint).\n  let license = await findAccountByStripeSession(session.id);\n\n  // Pool by email: a guest who buys with an email that ALREADY owns a granted\n  // key should top that key up, not get a second key with a split balance.\n  // Only when the session hasn't already resolved a license (retry-safe), and\n  // only for granted keys (a revoked key is dead — mint a fresh one instead).\n  let pooledIntoExisting = false;\n  if (!license) {\n    // An email can own several keys (e.g. a revoked key plus a live one), so\n    // scan all of them newest-first and pool into the most recent GRANTED key.\n    // (findFirst-by-email could hand back the dead revoked row and wrongly mint\n    // a second key with a split balance.)\n    const existingKeys = await getAccountKeysByEmail(\n      customerEmail.toLowerCase().trim()\n    );\n    const existing = existingKeys.find((k) => k.status === \"granted\");\n    if (existing) {\n      license = existing;\n      pooledIntoExisting = true;\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!license) {\n    // Generate a unique license key with collision check.\n    let licenseKey = \"\";\n    const maxAttempts = 10;\n    for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt++) {\n      licenseKey = generateLicenseKey();\n      const collision = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n      if (!collision) break;\n      console.warn(`License key collision detected, retrying... (${attempt})`);\n      if (attempt >= maxAttempts) {\n        throw new Error(\"Failed to generate unique license key after max attempts\");\n      }\n    }\n\n    const user = await getOrCreateUser(customerEmail, {\n      name: customerName,\n      ...(stripeCustomerId ? { stripeCustomerId } : {}),\n    });\n    if (!user) {\n      throw new Error(`Failed to create user for ${customerEmail}`);\n    }\n\n    try {\n      license = await insertAccountKey({\n        key: licenseKey,\n        email: customerEmail.toLowerCase().trim(),\n        userId: user.id,\n        stripeCustomerId,\n        stripeSessionId: session.id,\n        status: \"granted\",\n      });\n    } catch (insertError: unknown) {\n      // Concurrent webhook delivery inserted the row first (unique\n      // stripe_session_id): fall back to the existing row.\n      if (\n        insertError &&\n        typeof insertError === \"object\" &&\n        \"code\" in insertError &&\n        (insertError as { code: string }).code === \"23505\"\n      ) {\n        console.log(\"License already inserted by concurrent request\");\n        license = await findAccountByStripeSession(session.id);\n      } else {\n        console.error(\"Failed to store license key:\", insertError);\n        throw insertError;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (!license) {\n    throw new Error(`Failed to resolve minted license for session ${session.id}`);\n  }\n\n  // STEP 2: Grant the purchased credits (no included bundle on credit purchases).\n  // Idempotent on session.id, so safe to run on every delivery.\n  const grantResult = await grantCreditsForStripeEvent({\n    eventId,\n    eventType,\n    stripeObjectId: session.id,\n    userId: license.userId,\n    creditAmount,\n    sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n    sourceId: session.id,\n  });\n\n  // Retry/duplicate delivery: credits already granted for this session — don't\n  // re-send the email. (Pooling resolves the same license by email each retry,\n  // so this guard is what keeps a re-delivery from emailing twice.)\n  if (grantResult === \"duplicate\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Credit purchase already processed for session ${session.id}, skipping email`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 3: Email. When we pooled into an existing key, send a top-up receipt\n  // with the new balance (the buyer already has this key). A genuinely new key\n  // gets the mint email with its starting balance.\n  if (pooledIntoExisting) {\n    console.log(\n      `Pooled ${creditAmount} credits into existing key ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... for ${customerEmail}`\n    );\n\n    const newBalance = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n    const emailResult = await emailService.sendCreditTopUp({\n      customerName,\n      customerEmail,\n      licenseKey: license.key,\n      creditAmount,\n      newBalance,\n      productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n      supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n    });\n\n    if (!emailResult.success) {\n      console.error(`Failed to send top-up email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n    }\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(\n    `Minted license ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... with ${creditAmount} credits for ${customerEmail}`\n  );\n\n  const emailResult = await emailService.sendCreditMint({\n    customerName,\n    customerEmail,\n    licenseKey: license.key,\n    creditAmount,\n    productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n    supportEmail: \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n  });\n\n  if (!emailResult.success) {\n    console.error(`Failed to send mint email: ${emailResult.error}`);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Process a charge refund.\n *\n * - \"license\" purchases: revoke the associated license.\n * - \"credits\" purchases: deduct the granted credits from the balance.\n *\n * REFUND SCOPE (per purchase type):\n * - license: acts only on a FULL refund (amount_refunded === amount).\n * - credits: the 6% processing fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a\n *   policy-compliant credit refund refunds only the credit value — a *partial*\n *   Stripe refund. Acts once amount_refunded covers the credit portion\n *   (charge total minus the non-refundable fee).\n *\n * TRACE PATH:\n * Charge -> PaymentIntent -> Checkout Session -> license_keys.stripe_session_id\n *\n * IDEMPOTENCY:\n * License revocation is idempotent (\"revoked\" status). Credit deduction is\n * recorded in stripe_processed_events keyed by charge.id, so retried\n * charge.refunded events never double-deduct.\n */\nexport async function handleChargeRefunded(\n  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n  eventId: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  console.log(\n    `Processing refund for charge ${charge.id} (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount})`\n  );\n\n  // STEP 2: Get PaymentIntent ID from the charge\n  const paymentIntentId =\n    typeof charge.payment_intent === \"string\"\n      ? charge.payment_intent\n      : charge.payment_intent?.id;\n\n  if (!paymentIntentId) {\n    console.log(\n      \"No payment_intent on charge, skipping (likely not a Checkout purchase)\"\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 3: Find the Checkout Session associated with this PaymentIntent\n  const sessions = await stripe.checkout.sessions.list({\n    payment_intent: paymentIntentId,\n    limit: 1,\n  });\n\n  if (sessions.data.length === 0) {\n    console.log(\n      `No checkout session found for payment_intent ${paymentIntentId}`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const checkoutSession = sessions.data[0];\n  const purchaseType = checkoutSession.metadata?.purchase_type;\n\n  // STEP 4: Route by purchase type, applying the right \"is this refund\n  // actionable?\" rule for each.\n  if (purchaseType === \"credits\") {\n    // The 6% fee is a separate, non-refundable line item, so a credit refund\n    // refunds only the credit value — a partial Stripe refund. Act once the\n    // refunded amount covers the credit portion (charge total minus the fee).\n    const feeCents = parseInt(checkoutSession.metadata?.fee_cents || \"0\", 10);\n    const creditPortion = charge.amount - feeCents;\n    if (charge.amount_refunded < creditPortion) {\n      console.log(\n        `Refund ${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount} does not cover the credit portion (${creditPortion}) for credits session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n      );\n      return;\n    }\n    await handleCreditRefund(charge, checkoutSession, eventId);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  if (purchaseType !== \"license\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Refund for unknown purchase type (${purchaseType}), skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // License purchases: act only on a FULL refund.\n  if (charge.amount_refunded !== charge.amount) {\n    console.log(\n      `Partial refund (${charge.amount_refunded}/${charge.amount}) for license session ${checkoutSession.id}, skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  console.log(`Revoking license for checkout session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n\n  // STEP 5: Revoke the license in database\n  const license = await findAccountByStripeSession(checkoutSession.id);\n\n  if (!license) {\n    console.error(`License not found for session ${checkoutSession.id}`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  await refundCreditGrant({\n    sourceType: \"license_bundle\",\n    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n  });\n\n  // Already revoked - idempotent, just log and return\n  if (license.status === \"revoked\") {\n    console.log(`License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... already revoked`);\n    return;\n  }\n\n  // STEP 6: Update status to revoked\n  await updateAccountKey(license.id, { status: \"revoked\" });\n\n  console.log(\n    `License ${license.key.substring(0, 7)}... revoked due to full refund`\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Reverse a refunded credit-pack purchase.\n *\n * Looks up the original credit grant from the checkout session metadata and\n * atomically deducts it (clamped at 0). Idempotent via stripe_processed_events\n * keyed by charge.id, so webhook retries never double-deduct.\n */\nasync function handleCreditRefund(\n  charge: Stripe.Charge,\n  checkoutSession: Stripe.Checkout.Session,\n  eventId: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  // license_key is only present on top-ups; minted credit purchases carry none.\n  // The clawback is keyed on the checkout session id (the grant's source_id),\n  // so it works for both paths — license_key is informational only here.\n  const licenseKey = checkoutSession.metadata?.license_key;\n  const creditAmount = parseInt(\n    checkoutSession.metadata?.credit_amount || \"0\",\n    10\n  );\n\n  if (!creditAmount || creditAmount <= 0) {\n    console.error(\n      `Credit refund for session ${checkoutSession.id}: invalid metadata (credit_amount=${checkoutSession.metadata?.credit_amount}), skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const result = await refundCreditGrant({\n    sourceType: \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n    sourceId: checkoutSession.id,\n  });\n\n  if (result.status === \"duplicate\") {\n    console.log(\n      `Credit refund already processed for charge ${charge.id}, skipping`\n    );\n    return;\n  }\n\n  const licenseHint = licenseKey ? ` (license ${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}...)` : \"\";\n  console.log(\n    `Deducted ${result.refundedAmount} credits for refunded charge ${charge.id}${licenseHint}`\n  );\n}\n","numLines":617,"startLine":1,"totalLines":617}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2729c9c0-6660-4fb7-bc8c-0cc47b9dfd89","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

show credits the grants can't back, or vice versa.\n330\t */\n331\tasync function reconcileCreditBalance(\n332\t  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n333\t  userId: Throws on any failure.\n */\nexport async function provisionAccountKeyForEmail(email: string): Promise<AccountKeyRow> {\n  const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();\n\n  // Generate a unique license key with collision check\n  let key = \"\";\n  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {\n    key = generateLicenseKey();\n    const collision = await findAccountByKey(key);\n    if (!collision) break;\n    if (i === 4) {\n      throw new Error(\"Failed to generate unique license key\");\n    }\n  }\n\n  const name = normalizedEmail.split(\"@\")[0];\n  const userResult = await getOrCreateUser(normalizedEmail, { name });\n  if (!userResult) {\n    throw new Error(\"Failed to create user\");\n  }\n\n  const license = await insertAccountKey({\n    key,\n    email: normalizedEmail,\n    userId: userResult.id,\n    status: \"granted\",\n  });\n  if (!license) {\n    throw new Error(\"Failed to insert license key\");\n  }\n\n  await grantCreditLot({\n    userId: license.userId,\n    amount: INTERNAL_BUNDLE_CREDITS,\n    sourceType: \"internal_bundle\",\n    sourceId: license.id,\n  });\n\n  return license;\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Credit Balances and Grants\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n// A handle that can run queries: either the pooled `db` connection or a\n// transaction handle. Used so balance reads can join the caller's transaction\n// instead of opening a second connection (which sees a stale snapshot and ties\n// up the pool while row locks are held).\ntype DbExecutor = typeof db | Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0];\n\n// A grant still backs spendable balance when it has no expiry or hasn't expired\n// yet. This is the single source of that rule for the raw-SQL credit queries\n// below (balance read, spend, clawback) — keep it here so expiry semantics live\n// in one place. The Drizzle-builder variant (using creditGrants.expiresAt) is\n// expressed inline where the query is built with the query builder.\nconst ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY = sql`(expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())`;\n\n/**\n * Authoritative spendable balance: the SUM of remaining_amount across a\n * license's still-active grants. The credit_grants rows are the source of\n * truth; credit_balances is only a denormalized cache of this value. Reading\n * via the passed executor lets callers inside a FOR UPDATE transaction get a\n * consistent, locked view rather than a stale snapshot from the outer pool.\n */\nasync function getActiveGrantsTotal(\n  executor: DbExecutor,\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const result = await executor.execute<{ total: string | null }>(sql`\n    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(remaining_amount), 0) AS total\n    FROM credit_grants\n    WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n      AND status = 'active'\n      AND remaining_amount > 0\n      AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n  `);\n  return Number(result.rows[0]?.total ?? 0);\n}\n\n/**\n * Force the credit_balances cache to equal the authoritative grant total,\n * reconciling any drift between the two. Returns the reconciled balance. This\n * is the single place that heals divergence (e.g. a legacy balance row with no\n * matching grants, or a partially-applied transaction) so the cache can never\n * permanently show credits the grants can't back, or vice versa.\n */\nasync function reconcileCreditBalance(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const total = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, userId);\n  await tx\n    .insert(creditBalances)\n    .values({ userId, balance: total.toString() })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: creditBalances.userId,\n      set: {\n        balance: total.toString(),\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    });\n  return total;\n}\n\nasync function incrementCreditBalance(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  userId: string,\n  amount: number\n): Promise<number> {\n  const [row] = await tx\n    .insert(creditBalances)\n    .values({ userId, balance: amount.toString() })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: creditBalances.userId,\n      set: {\n        balance: sql`${creditBalances.balance} + ${amount}`,\n        updatedAt: new Date(),\n      },\n    })\n    .returning({ balance: creditBalances.balance });\n\n  return Number(row.balance);\n}\n\n// Every credit grant expires one year after it is created (OpenRouter model):\n// the ToS reserves the right to expire unused credits 365 days after purchase.\n// Stamped here so it covers every insert path (paid packs, minted keys, admin\n// grants); enforcement is the lazy `expires_at > now()` filter on reads/spends.\nconst CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS = 365 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;\n\nasync function grantCreditLotInTransaction(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  data: CreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n  const expiresAt = new Date(Date.now() + CREDIT_GRANT_TTL_MS);\n  const [grantRow] = await tx\n    .insert(creditGrants)\n    .values({\n      userId: data.userId,\n      sourceType: data.sourceType,\n      sourceId: data.sourceId,\n      originalAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n      remainingAmount: data.amount.toString(),\n      refundedAmount: \"0\",\n      status: \"active\",\n      expiresAt,\n    })\n    .onConflictDoNothing({\n      target: [creditGrants.sourceType, creditGrants.sourceId],\n    })\n    .returning({ id: creditGrants.id });\n\n  if (!grantRow) {\n    // Read inside the transaction so the balance reflects the locked, in-tx\n    // view rather than a stale snapshot from a second pooled connection.\n    const balance = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, data.userId);\n    return { status: \"duplicate\", balance };\n  }\n\n  const balance = await incrementCreditBalance(tx, data.userId, data.amount);\n  return { status: \"processed\", balance };\n}\n\nexport async function grantCreditLot(\n  data: CreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; balance: number }> {\n  return db.transaction((tx) => grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, data));\n}\n\nexport async function grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\n  data: StripeCreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<\"processed\" | \"duplicate\"> {\n  const insertedEvent = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const [eventRow] = await tx\n      .insert(stripeProcessedEvents)","numLines":330,"startLine":90,"totalLines":1024}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"5fd26be6-8217-45a7-b244-385eca666f5c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{\n  index,\n  numeric,\n  pgTable,\n  text,\n  timestamp,\n  uniqueIndex,\n  uuid,\n} from \"drizzle-orm/pg-core\";\nimport { relations } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { user } from \"./auth\";\n\nexport const creditGrants = pgTable(\n  \"credit_grants\",\n  {\n    id: uuid(\"id\").defaultRandom().primaryKey(),\n    userId: text(\"user_id\")\n      .notNull()\n      .references(() => user.id, { onDelete: \"cascade\" }),\n    sourceType: text(\"source_type\").notNull(),\n    sourceId: text(\"source_id\").notNull(),\n    originalAmount: numeric(\"original_amount\", { precision: 20, scale: 2 })\n      .notNull(),\n    remainingAmount: numeric(\"remaining_amount\", { precision: 20, scale: 2 })\n      .notNull(),\n    refundedAmount: numeric(\"refunded_amount\", { precision: 20, scale: 2 })\n      .notNull()\n      .default(\"0\"),\n    status: text(\"status\").notNull().default(\"active\"),\n    // When this grant's credits expire and stop counting toward the spendable\n    // balance. Null means never expires (e.g. trial credits). Stripe-only license\n    // rows (no polar_license_key_id) are unaffected.\n    uniqueIndex(\"idx_account_keys_polar_license_key_id\").on(\n      table.polarLicenseKeyId\n    ),\n    index(\"idx_account_keys_email\").on(table.email),\n    uniqueIndex(\"idx_account_keys_key\").on(table.key),\n    index(\"idx_account_keys_user_id\").on(table.userId),\n    index(\"idx_account_keys_polar_customer\").on(table.polarCustomerId),\n    index(\"idx_account_keys_stripe_customer\").on(table.stripeCustomerId),\n  ]\n);\n\n// Relations\n\nexport const accountKeysRelations = relations(accountKeys, ({ one, many }) => ({\n  user: one(user, {\n    fields: [accountKeys.userId],\n    references: [user.id],\n  }),\n  deviceValidations: many(deviceValidations),\n}));\n","numLines":58,"startLine":1,"totalLines":58}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"00ec18a3-c807-4bec-aa20-5c6824ef4661","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

We deliberately do NOT filter\n450\t    // on remaining_amount > 0: a fully-consumed pack still has to be clawed back,\n451\t    // otherwise spending the pack first (so a refund can't hand back unused paid\n452\t    // credits — see spendCreditGrantsByProvenance / #872) would let a refund of a\n453\t    // fully-spent pack reclaim nothing, leaving the user with the paid usage for\n454\t    // free PLUS the cash refund (the inverse money-loss bug).\n455\t    const target = await tx.execute<{\n456\t      id: string;\n457\t      user_id: string;\n458\t      original_amount: string;\n459\t      refunded_amount: string;\n460\t    }>(sql`\n461\t      SELECT id, user_id, original_amount, refunded_amount\n462\t      FROM credit_grants\n463\t      WHERE source_type = ${data.sourceType}\n464\t        AND source_id = ${data.sourceId}\n465\t      FOR UPDATE\n466\t    `);\n467\t\n468\t    const grant = target.rows[0];\n469\t    if (!grant) {\n470\t      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n471\t    }\n472\t\n473\t    // The amount this refund reclaims is the grant's full purchased value that\n474\t    // has not already been refunded. A refunded grant (refunded_amount already\n475\t    // covers original_amount) is a no-op / duplicate webhook delivery.\n476\t    const originalAmount = Number(grant.original_amount);\n477\t    const alreadyRefunded = Number(grant.refunded_amount);\n478\t    const clawback = originalAmount - alreadyRefunded;\n479\t    if (clawback <= 0) {\n480\t      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n481\t    }\n482\t\n483\t    // A full refund must remove the FULL purchased value from the license's\n484\t    // spendable balance, regardless of which grant happened to be spent for the\n485\t    // matching usage. Clamped at the license's current active total so a user who\n486\t    // already burned the credits doesn't go negative, but no value escapes:\n487\t    //   spend order + refund clawback are reconciled against the SAME running\n488\t    //   total, so neither bundle-first nor pack-first ordering can leak money.\n489\t    let toClawback = Math.min(\n490\t      clawback,\n491\t      await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, grant.user_id)\n492\t    );\n493\t\n494\t    // Draw the clawback down from the license's active, unexpired grants,\n495\t    // starting with the refunded grant itself, then the same oldest-first order\n496\t    // used when spending (soonest-to-expire first). The clawback total is\n497\t    // clamped at the license's current active balance, and both spend and\n498\t    // clawback reconcile against that same running total, so no ordering can\n499\t    // leak money. Expired grants are skipped: they no longer back any spendable\n500\t    // balance, so clawing them back would remove value that was never available.\n501\t    const drawdown = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n502\t      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n503\t      FROM credit_grants\n504\t      WHERE user_id = ${grant.user_id}\n505\t        AND remaining_amount > 0\n506\t        AND status = 'active'\n507\t        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n508\t      ORDER BY\n509\t        CASE WHEN id = ${grant.id} THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,\n510\t        expires_at ASC,\n511\t        created_at ASC,\n512\t        id\n513\t      FOR UPDATE\n514\t    `);\n515\t\n516\t    for (const row of drawdown.rows) {\n517\t      if (toClawback <= 0) break;\n518\t      const rowRemaining = Number(row.remaining_amount);\n519\t      const deduction = Math.min(rowRemaining, toClawback);\n520\t      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n521\t      const newRemaining = rowRemaining - deduction;\n522\t      await tx\n523\t        .update(creditGrants)\n524\t        .set({\n525\t          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n526\t          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n527\t          updatedAt: new Date(),\n528\t        })\n529\t        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, row.id));\n530\t      toClawback -= deduction;\n531\t    }\n532\t\n533\t    // Mark the refunded grant as refunded and record the full reclaimed value on\n534\t    // it (even when the credits were physically drawn from other grants), so the\n535\t    // grant's lifetime is correct and a duplicate refund webhook is a no-op.\n536\t    await tx\n537\t      .update(creditGrants)\n538\t      .set({\n539\t        remainingAmount: \"0\",\n540\t        refundedAmount: (alreadyRefunded + clawback).toString(),\n541\t        status: \"refunded\",\n542\t        updatedAt: new Date(),\n543\t      })\n544\t      .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n545\t\n546\t    // Reconcile the cache to the authoritative grant total, healing any drift.\n547\t    await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, grant.user_id);\n548\t    return {\n549\t      status: \"processed\",\n550\t      refundedAmount: clawback,\n551\t    };\n552\t  });\n553\t}\n554\t\n555\texport async function spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(\n556\t  userId: string,\n557\t  amount: number\n558\t): Promise<{ balance: number; deductedAmount: number }> {\n559\t  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n560\t    const result = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n561\t      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n562\t      FROM credit_grants\n563\t      WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n564\t        AND remaining_amount > 0\n565\t        AND status = 'active'\n566\t        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n567\t      -- Spend OLDEST-FIRST: soonest-to-expire grants are consumed before grants\n568\t      -- that still have time on them (and never-expiring grants last), so a user\n569\t      -- naturally burns down credits before they lapse. This replaces the older\n570\t      -- provenance (paid-pack-first) order; refund safety is now preserved a\n571\t      -- different way — refundCreditGrant clamps every clawback at the license's\n572\t      -- current active balance and reconciles against the same running total\n573\t      -- (#872 / spec §6), so neither spend nor clawback ordering can leak money\n574\t      -- regardless of which grant a given unit of usage drew from. Expired\n575\t      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n576\t      ORDER BY\n577\t        expires_at ASC,\n578\t        created_at ASC,\n579\t        id\n580\t      FOR UPDATE\n581\t    `);\n582\t\n583\t    let remainingToDeduct = amount;\n584\t    let deductedAmount = 0;\n585\t\n586\t    for (let index = 0; index < result.rows.length; index++) {\n587\t      if (remainingToDeduct <= 0) break;\n588\t\n589\t      const grant = result.rows[index];\n590\t      const grantRemaining = Number(grant.remaining_amount);\n591\t      const deduction = Math.min(grantRemaining, remainingToDeduct);\n592\t\n593\t      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n594\t\n595\t      const newRemaining = grantRemaining - deduction;\n596\t      await tx\n597\t        .update(creditGrants)\n598\t        .set({\n599\t          remainingAmount: newRemaining.toString(),\n600\t          status: newRemaining === 0 ? \"spent\" : \"active\",\n601\t          updatedAt: new Date(),\n602\t        })\n603\t        .where(eq(creditGrants.id, grant.id));\n604\t\n605\t      remainingToDeduct -= deduction;\n606\t      deductedAmount += deduction;\n607\t    }\n608\t\n609\t    // Reconcile the cached balance to the authoritative grant total in the same\n610\t    // transaction (and same locked view). This both (a) reads via `tx` instead\n611\t    // of a second pooled connection, and (b) self-heals any drift between the\n612\t    // credit_balances cache and the grant rows, so the cache can never report\n613\t    // spendable credits the grants can't actually back, or vice versa.\n614\t    const balance = await reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId);\n615\t\n616\t    return { balance, deductedAmount };\n617\t  });\n618\t}\n619\t\n620\t/**\n621\t * Atomically deduct credits from source-tracked grants, flooring at zero.\n622\t *\n623\t * Returns the new cached aggregate balance. The public API response shape is\n624\t * preserved, but the underlying grant rows record which source was consumed.\n625\t */\n626\texport async function deductCreditBalance(userId: string, amount: number): Promise<number> {\n627\t  const result = await spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(userId, amount);\n628\t  return result.balance;\n629\t}\n630\t\n631\t/**\n632\t * Spendable credit balance for a single license.\n633\t *\n634\t * The credit_grants rows are the source of truth; credit_balances is only a\n635\t * cache of their summed remaining_amount. To avoid ever reporting credits the\n636\t * user cannot actually consume (or hiding credits they can), this returns the\n637\t * authoritative grant total and lazily heals the cache when the two have\n638\t * drifted. The cheap read path (cache hit that already matches) does no write.\n639\t */\n640\texport async function getCreditBalance(userId: string): Promise<number> {\n641\t  const [cachedRow, grantsTotal] = await Promise.all([\n642\t    db.query.creditBalances.findFirst({\n643\t      where: eq(creditBalances.userId, userId),\n644\t    }),\n645\t    getActiveGrantsTotal(db, userId),\n646\t  ]);\n647\t\n648\t  const cached = cachedRow ? Number(cachedRow.balance) : null;\n649\t  if (cached !== grantsTotal) {\n650\t    // Drift detected (e.g. a legacy balance row with no matching grants, or a\n651\t    // partially-applied write): heal the cache to the authoritative total.\n652\t    await db.transaction((tx) => reconcileCreditBalance(tx, userId));\n653\t  }\n654\t\n655\t  return grantsTotal;\n656\t}\n657\t\n658\t/**\n659\t * Spendable credit balances for many accounts at once (dashboard / admin list).\n660\t *\n661\t * Like {@link getCreditBalance}, the credit_grants rows are the source of truth\n662\t * and credit_balances is only a cache. This reads the authoritative grant total\n663\t * (SUM of remaining_amount across each account's still-active grants) directly,\n664\t * so an account whose cached balance has drifted above its true grant total never\n665\t * shows phantom credits on the dashboard. Accounts with no active grants default\n666\t * to 0. (The cache still self-heals on the next single-account ledger\n667\t * operation; this read path simply never relies on it.)\n668\t */\n669\texport async function getCreditBalancesForUsers(userIds: string[]): Promise<Map<string, number>> {\n670\t  const map = new Map<string, number>();\n671\t  if (userIds.length === 0) return map;\n672\t  // Default every requested id to 0 so accounts with no active grants are\n673\t  // explicitly present in the map."}]},"uuid":"5deae15f-4c7f-4baa-b565-889029d26175","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:40:35.756Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/src/lib/db-layer.ts","content":"export async function grantCreditsForStripeEvent(\n  data: StripeCreditGrantInsert\n): Promise<\"processed\" | \"duplicate\"> {\n  const insertedEvent = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const [eventRow] = await tx\n      .insert(stripeProcessedEvents)\n      .values({\n        eventId: data.eventId,\n        eventType: data.eventType,\n        stripeObjectId: data.stripeObjectId,\n      })\n      .onConflictDoNothing({ target: stripeProcessedEvents.stripeObjectId })\n      .returning({ eventId: stripeProcessedEvents.eventId });\n\n    if (!eventRow) {\n      return null;\n    }\n\n    await grantCreditLotInTransaction(tx, {\n      userId: data.userId,\n      amount: data.creditAmount,\n      sourceType: data.sourceType ?? \"stripe_credit_pack\",\n      sourceId: data.sourceId ?? data.stripeObjectId,\n    });\n\n    return eventRow;\n  });\n\n  return insertedEvent ? \"processed\" : \"duplicate\";\n}\n\nexport async function refundCreditGrant(\n  data: CreditGrantRefund\n): Promise<{ status: \"processed\" | \"duplicate\"; refundedAmount: number }> {\n  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    // Resolve the refunded purchase by provenance. {"parentUuid":"6c66047e-4faf-47c1-a466-f450ee1b8dfd","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a370a3bfb6c20dc02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AJPE7LEUkdTJhssJwnaGvX","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { resend, DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL } from \"@/lib/clients/resend\";\n2\timport {\n3\t  licenseEmailHtml,\n4\t  licenseEmailText,\n5\t  type LicenseEmailData,\n6\t} from \"@/lib/templates/license-email\";\n7\timport {\n8\t  welcomeEmailHtml,\n9\t  welcomeEmailText,\n10\t  type WelcomeEmailData,\n11\t} from \"@/lib/templates/welcome-email\";\n12\timport {\n13\t  creditMintEmailHtml,\n14\t  creditMintEmailText,\n15\t  type CreditMintEmailData,\n16\t} from \"@/lib/templates/credit-mint-email\";\n17\timport {\n18\t  creditTopUpEmailHtml,\n19\t  creditTopUpEmailText,\n20\t  type CreditTopUpEmailData,\n21\t} from \"@/lib/templates/credit-topup-email\";\n22\timport { logSentEmail } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n23\t\n24\texport interface EmailResult {\n25\t  success: boolean;\n26\t  data?: any;\n27\t  error?: string;\n28\t}\n29\t\n30\t/**\n31\t * Error thrown when the Resend API resolves with an error object instead of\n32\t * throwing. Carries the Resend error name/statusCode so we can decide whether\n33\t * the failure is worth retrying.\n34\t */\n35\tclass ResendSendError extends Error {\n36\t  readonly retryable: boolean;\n37\t\n38\t  constructor(message: string, name: string, statusCode: number | null) {\n39\t    super(message);\n40\t    this.name = \"ResendSendError\";\n41\t    this.retryable = isRetryableResendError(name, statusCode);\n42\t  }\n43\t}\n44\t\n45\t/**\n46\t * Decide whether a Resend failure is transient (worth retrying) or permanent.\n47\t *\n48\t * Permanent failures (invalid recipient/domain, validation errors, auth errors,\n49\t * suppressed recipients) will never succeed on retry, so we fail fast instead of\n50\t * burning the webhook request thread on backoff sleeps.\n51\t */\n52\tfunction isRetryableResendError(\n53\t  name: string | undefined,\n54\t  statusCode: number | null,\n55\t): boolean {\n56\t  // Rate limits and server-side errors are transient.\n57\t  if (\n58\t    name === \"rate_limit_exceeded\" ||\n59\t    name === \"internal_server_error\" ||\n60\t    name === \"application_error\"\n61\t  ) {\n62\t    return true;\n63\t  }\n64\t\n65\t  // Any 5xx (or unknown/missing status) is treated as transient.\n66\t  if (statusCode == null) return true;\n67\t  return statusCode >= 500;\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\texport class EmailService {\n71\t  private static instance: EmailService;\n72\t  private readonly maxRetries = 3;\n73\t  // Base backoff. Kept small because retries run inline inside the Stripe\n74\t  // webhook request; worst-case total sleep across attempts is bounded\n75\t  // (250ms + 500ms = 750ms) to stay well under Stripe's webhook timeout.\n76\t  private readonly retryDelay = 250;\n77\t\n78\t  private constructor() {}\n79\t\n80\t  public static getInstance(): EmailService {\n81\t    if (!EmailService.instance) {\n82\t      EmailService.instance = new EmailService();\n83\t    }\n84\t\n85\t    return EmailService.instance;\n86\t  }\n87\t\n88\t  /**\n89\t   * Send license key email with retry logic\n90\t   */\n91\t  async sendLicenseKey(data: LicenseEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n92\t    const subject = `Your ${data.productName} License Key`;\n93\t    return this.sendWithRetry(\"license\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n94\t      resend.emails.send({\n95\t        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n96\t        to: data.customerEmail,\n97\t        subject,\n98\t        html: licenseEmailHtml(data),\n99\t        text: licenseEmailText(data),\n100\t      }),\n101\t    );\n102\t  }\n103\t\n104\t  /**\n105\t   * Send the onboarding welcome email used after someone submits the download form.\n106\t   */\n107\t  async sendWelcomeEmail(data: WelcomeEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n108\t    const subject = `Welcome to ${data.productName}`;\n109\t    return this.sendWithRetry(\"welcome\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n110\t      resend.emails.send({\n111\t        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n112\t        to: data.customerEmail,\n113\t        subject,\n114\t        html: welcomeEmailHtml(data),\n115\t        text: welcomeEmailText(data),\n116\t      }),\n117\t    );\n118\t  }\n119\t\n120\t  /**\n121\t   * Send the mint email: a guest bought credits with no key, so we created one.\n122\t   * Delivers the new key and its starting balance.\n123\t   */\n124\t  async sendCreditMint(data: CreditMintEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> { resend, DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL } from \"@/lib/clients/resend\";\nimport {\n  licenseEmailHtml,\n  licenseEmailText,\n  type LicenseEmailData,\n} from \"@/lib/templates/license-email\";\nimport {\n  welcomeEmailHtml,\n  welcomeEmailText,\n  type WelcomeEmailData,\n} from \"@/lib/templates/welcome-email\";\nimport {\n  creditMintEmailHtml,\n  creditMintEmailText,\n  type CreditMintEmailData,\n} from \"@/lib/templates/credit-mint-email\";\nimport {\n  creditTopUpEmailHtml,\n  creditTopUpEmailText,\n  type CreditTopUpEmailData,\n} from \"@/lib/templates/credit-topup-email\";\nimport { logSentEmail } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\nexport interface EmailResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  data?: any;\n  error?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Error thrown when the Resend API resolves with an error object instead of\n * throwing. Carries the Resend error name/statusCode so we can decide whether\n * the failure is worth retrying.\n */\nclass ResendSendError extends Error {\n  readonly retryable: boolean;\n\n  constructor(message: string, name: string, statusCode: number | null) {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = \"ResendSendError\";\n    this.retryable = isRetryableResendError(name, statusCode);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Decide whether a Resend failure is transient (worth retrying) or permanent.\n *\n * Permanent failures (invalid recipient/domain, validation errors, auth errors,\n * suppressed recipients) will never succeed on retry, so we fail fast instead of\n * burning the webhook request thread on backoff sleeps.\n */\nfunction isRetryableResendError(\n  name: string | undefined,\n  statusCode: number | null,\n): boolean {\n  // Rate limits and server-side errors are transient.\n  if (\n    name === \"rate_limit_exceeded\" ||\n    name === \"internal_server_error\" ||\n    name === \"application_error\"\n  ) {\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  // Any 5xx (or unknown/missing status) is treated as transient.\n  if (statusCode == null) return true;\n  return statusCode >= 500;\n}\n\nexport class EmailService {\n  private static instance: EmailService;\n  private readonly maxRetries = 3;\n  // Base backoff. Kept small because retries run inline inside the Stripe\n  // webhook request; worst-case total sleep across attempts is bounded\n  // (250ms + 500ms = 750ms) to stay well under Stripe's webhook timeout.\n  private readonly retryDelay = 250;\n\n  private constructor() {}\n\n  public static getInstance(): EmailService {\n    if (!EmailService.instance) {\n      EmailService.instance = new EmailService();\n    }\n\n    return EmailService.instance;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send license key email with retry logic\n   */\n  async sendLicenseKey(data: LicenseEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `Your ${data.productName} License Key`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"license\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: licenseEmailHtml(data),\n        text: licenseEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send the onboarding welcome email used after someone submits the download form.\n   */\n  async sendWelcomeEmail(data: WelcomeEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `Welcome to ${data.productName}`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"welcome\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: welcomeEmailHtml(data),\n        text: welcomeEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send the mint email: a guest bought credits with no key, so we created one.\n   * Delivers the new key and its starting balance.\n   */\n  async sendCreditMint(data: CreditMintEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `Your ${data.productName} key and credits`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"credit-mint\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: creditMintEmailHtml(data),\n        text: creditMintEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send the top-up receipt: credits were added to an existing license key.\n   */\n  async sendCreditTopUp(data: CreditTopUpEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `${data.creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits added`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"credit-topup\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: creditTopUpEmailHtml(data),\n        text: creditTopUpEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Shared send-with-retry loop.\n   *\n   * Treats a resolved `{ error }` object from the Resend SDK as a failure (the\n   * SDK does NOT throw on API-level errors), so transient failures actually\n   * retry and permanent failures surface as `{ success: false }` instead of a\n   * false positive. Permanent (non-retryable) errors abort immediately so the\n   * caller's request thread is not blocked on backoff sleeps.\n   */\n  private async sendWithRetry(\n    kind: \"license\" | \"welcome\" | \"credit-mint\" | \"credit-topup\",\n    customerEmail: string,\n    subject: string,\n    send: () => Promise<{ data: unknown; error: unknown }>,\n  ): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    let lastError: Error | undefined;\n\n    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= this.maxRetries; attempt++) {\n      try {\n        console.log(\n          `Sending ${kind} email to ${customerEmail} (attempt ${attempt}/${this.maxRetries})`,\n        );\n\n        const result = await send();\n\n        // The Resend SDK resolves to { data, error } and does NOT throw on\n        // API-level failures, so an error object must be inspected explicitly.\n        if (result.error) {\n          const err = result.error as {\n            message?: string;\n            name?: string;\n            statusCode?: number | null;\n          };\n          throw new ResendSendError(\n            err.message || `Resend returned an error sending ${kind} email`,\n            err.name ?? \"\",\n            err.statusCode ?? null,\n          );\n        }\n\n        console.log(`${capitalize(kind)} email sent successfully to ${customerEmail}`);\n\n        await this.safeLogSentEmail({\n          recipient: customerEmail,\n          emailType: kind,\n          subject,\n          providerMessageId: (result.data as { id?: string })?.id ?? null,\n          status: \"sent\",\n        });\n\n        return { success: true, data: result.data };\n      } catch (error) {\n        lastError = error as Error;\n        console.error(\n          `Failed to send ${kind} email (attempt ${attempt}):`,\n          error,\n        );\n\n        // Don't retry permanent failures (bad recipient/domain, validation,\n        // auth) — they will never succeed and would only block on backoff.\n        if (error instanceof ResendSendError && !error.retryable) {\n          break;\n        }\n\n        // If not the last attempt, wait before retrying (exponential backoff).\n        if (attempt < this.maxRetries) {\n          const delay = this.retryDelay * Math.pow(2, attempt - 1);\n\n          console.log(`Retrying in ${delay}ms...`);\n          await this.sleep(delay);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    await this.safeLogSentEmail({\n      recipient: customerEmail,\n      emailType: kind,\n      subject,\n      status: \"failed\",\n      errorMessage: lastError?.message ?? null,\n    });\n\n    return {\n      success: false,\n      error:\n        lastError?.message || \"Failed to send email after multiple attempts\",\n    };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Best-effort audit log of one logical email. A logging failure must never\n   * affect email delivery or its result, so this swallows and console.errors —\n   * both the success and failure paths route through here so the behavior can't\n   * drift between them.\n   */\n  private async safeLogSentEmail(\n    data: Parameters<typeof logSentEmail>[0],\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await logSentEmail(data);\n    } catch (logError) {\n      console.error(\n        `Failed to log ${data.status} ${data.emailType} email:`,\n        logError,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Sleep for a specified duration\n   */\n  private sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {\n    return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));\n  }\n}\n\nfunction capitalize(value: string): string {\n  return value.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + value.slice(1);\n}\n\n// Export singleton instance\nexport const emailService = EmailService.getInstance();\n","numLines":275,"startLine":1,"totalLines":275}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"6c66047e-4faf-47c1-a466-f450ee1b8dfd","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"5b57604c-9f1e-4eb2-877d-2bf17eda5353","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a370a3bfb6c20dc02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01EdZjV8enHR9YA3BKonFMLW","type":"tool_result","content":"src/lib/db-layer.ts:260:  const userResult = await getOrCreateUser(normalizedEmail, { name });\nsrc/lib/db-layer.ts:932:export async function getOrCreateUser(\n---\n  });\n}\n\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n// Users\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nexport async function getOrCreateUser(\n  email: string,\n  metadata?: { name?: string; stripeCustomerId?: string; polarCustomerId?: string }\n): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  // Check if user exists\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.email, email.toLowerCase().trim()),\n  });\n  if (existing) return { id: existing.id, email: existing.email };\n\n  // Create via Better Auth's user table.\n  // emailVerified stays false: this address comes from a Stripe/Polar checkout\n  // or an admin/import grant, so the mailbox owner has never proven control of\n  // it. The magic-link plugin flips emailVerified to true on the first\n  // successful sign-in, which is the only point where ownership is verified.\n  const userId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  const [newUser] = await db\n    .insert(user)\n    .values({\n      id: userId,\n      name: metadata?.name ?? email.split(\"@\")[0],\n      email: email.toLowerCase().trim(),\n      emailVerified: false,\n    })\n    .returning();\n  if (!newUser) return null;\n\n  // Also insert an account record so the user can sign in via magic link\n  await db.insert(account).values({\n    id: `magic-link-${userId}`,\n    accountId: userId,\n    providerId: \"magic-link\",\n    userId: userId,\n    createdAt: new Date(),\n    updatedAt: new Date(),\n  });\n\n  return { id: newUser.id, email: newUser.email };\n}\n\nexport async function getUserById(id: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.id, id),\n  });\n  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n}\n\nexport async function getUserByEmail(email: string): Promise<UserResult | null> {\n  const existing = await db.query.user.findFirst({\n    where: eq(user.email, email.toLowerCase().trim()),\n  });\n  return existing ? { id: existing.id, email: existing.email } : null;\n}\n\nexport async function getUsersByIds(ids: string[]): Promise<Map<string, UserResult>> {\n  if (ids.length === 0) return new Map();\n  const rows = await db.query.user.findMany({\n    where: inArray(user.id, ids),\n  });\n  const map = new Map<string, UserResult>();\n  for (const row of rows) {\n    map.set(row.id, { id: row.id, email: row.email });\n  }\n  return map;\n}\n\n/**\n * Move a customer to a new email. {"parentUuid":"4569d61a-1670-4215-80e5-fa1927599d6d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"77ec06fb-aafc-4d6b-b92c-3296ca279672","agentId":"a370a3bfb6c20dc02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016w5BcKYh3TGBMfrL5ZPhk4","type":"tool_result","content":"=== purchase-success page ===\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from \"react\";\nimport { m } from \"framer-motion\";\nimport { Button } from \"@heroui/button\";\nimport { CheckCircle, ArrowRight } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useSearchParams } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { usePostHog } from \"posthog-js/react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\n\nimport { Link } from \"@/src/i18n/navigation\";\n\nfunction PurchaseSuccessContent() {\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const posthog = usePostHog();\n  const t = useTranslations(\"purchaseSuccess\");\n  const [checkoutId, setCheckoutId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [eventCaptured, setEventCaptured] = useState(false);\n\n  // Credits purchased, passed through the Stripe success_url. Used only to\n  // personalize the confirmation copy — never trusted for entitlement.\n  const creditsParam = searchParams?.get(\"credits\");\n  const credits = creditsParam ? Number.parseInt(creditsParam, 10) : NaN;\n  const hasCredits = Number.isFinite(credits) && credits > 0;\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    // Support both Stripe (session_id) and legacy Polar (checkout_id) params\n    const id =\n      searchParams?.get(\"session_id\") || searchParams?.get(\"checkout_id\");\n\n    if (id) {\n      setCheckoutId(id);\n    }\n  }, [searchParams]);\n\n  // Separate effect to capture purchase event only when PostHog is ready\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (checkoutId && posthog && !eventCaptured) {\n      posthog.capture(\"purchase_completed\", { session_id: checkoutId });\n      setEventCaptured(true);\n    }\n  }, [checkoutId, posthog, eventCaptured]);\n\n  const steps = [\n    { title: t(\"step1Title\"), description: t(\"step1Desc\") },\n    { title: t(\"step2Title\"), description: t(\"step2Desc\") },\n    { title: t(\"step3Title\"), description: t(\"step3Desc\") },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b purchase-success page ===\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from \"react\";\nimport { m } from \"framer-motion\";\nimport { Button } from \"@heroui/button\";\nimport { CheckCircle, ArrowRight } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useSearchParams } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { usePostHog } from \"posthog-js/react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\n\nimport { Link } from \"@/src/i18n/navigation\";\n\nfunction PurchaseSuccessContent() {\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const posthog = usePostHog();\n  const t = useTranslations(\"purchaseSuccess\");\n  const [checkoutId, setCheckoutId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [eventCaptured, setEventCaptured] = useState(false);\n\n  // Credits purchased, passed through the Stripe success_url. Used only to\n  // personalize the confirmation copy — never trusted for entitlement.\n  const creditsParam = searchParams?.get(\"credits\");\n  const credits = creditsParam ? Number.parseInt(creditsParam, 10) : NaN;\n  const hasCredits = Number.isFinite(credits) && credits > 0;\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    // Support both Stripe (session_id) and legacy Polar (checkout_id) params\n    const id =\n      searchParams?.get(\"session_id\") || searchParams?.get(\"checkout_id\");\n\n    if (id) {\n      setCheckoutId(id);\n    }\n  }, [searchParams]);\n\n  // Separate effect to capture purchase event only when PostHog is ready\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (checkoutId && posthog && !eventCaptured) {\n      posthog.capture(\"purchase_completed\", { session_id: checkoutId });\n      setEventCaptured(true);\n    }\n  }, [checkoutId, posthog, eventCaptured]);\n\n  const steps = [\n    { title: t(\"step1Title\"), description: t(\"step1Desc\") },\n    { title: t(\"step2Title\"), description: t(\"step2Desc\") },\n    { title: t(\"step3Title\"), description: t(\"step3Desc\") },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-gray-900 via-purple-900/10 to-gray-900 flex items-center justify-center px-7 py-20\">\n      <m.div\n        animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}\n        className=\"max-w-xl w-full text-center\"\n        initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}\n        transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}\n      >\n        <m.div\n          animate={{ scale: 1 }}\n          className=\"inline-flex items-center justify-center w-[82px] h-[82px] bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500 to-emerald-500 rounded-full mb-9 shadow-[0_12px_40px_-8px_rgba(16,185,129,0.5)]\"","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4569d61a-1670-4215-80e5-fa1927599d6d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"40b00ac3-ee4d-4fd5-b189-0d68f3911dfa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"103cde84-18ec-4036-bbbf-c8f26cc8bae4","agentId":"a370a3bfb6c20dc02","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01CMujcXmAmbXyyg2uPAeCzf","type":"tool_result","content":"import assert from \"node:assert/strict\";\nimport test from \"node:test\";\n\nimport {\n  MIN_CREDIT_DOLLARS,\n  MAX_CREDIT_DOLLARS,\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"../app/api/checkout/credits/validation\";\n\ntest(\"accepts whole-dollar amounts within bounds\", () => {\n  assert.equal(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(MIN_CREDIT_DOLLARS), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(10), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(37), null);\n  assert.equal(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(MAX_CREDIT_DOLLARS), null);\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects amounts below the minimum\", () => {\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(MIN_CREDIT_DOLLARS - 1), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(0), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(-5), null);\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects amounts above the maximum\", () => {\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(MAX_CREDIT_DOLLARS + 1), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(10000), null);\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects non-integer dollar amounts\", () => {\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(5.5), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(19.99), null);\n});\n\ntest(\"rejects non-finite / non-number amounts\", () => {\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(Number.NaN), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(Number.POSITIVE_INFINITY), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(\"10\"), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(undefined), null);\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(null), null);\n  // 1e999 parses to Infinity from JSON, the classic overflow payload.\n  const parsed = JSON.parse('{\"amount\":1e999}') as { amount: unknown };\n  assert.notEqual(validateCreditPurchaseAmount(parsed.amount), null);\n});\n\ntest(\"credits = amount * 1000, no free bundle\", () => {\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(5).creditAmount, 5000);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(10).creditAmount, 10000);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(20).creditAmount, 20000);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(37).creditAmount, 37000);\n});\n\ntest(\"credit line item is amount * 100 cents\", () => {\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(5).creditCents, 500);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(500).creditCents, 50000);\n});\n\ntest(\"processing fee is round(6% of credit cents)\", () => {\n  // 6% of $5 = $0.30 -> 30 cents\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(5).feeCents, 30);\n  // 6% of $10 = $0.60 -> 60 cents\n=== all test files ===\ncredit-purchase-validation.test.ts\ndownload-ip-validation.test.ts\ngeolocation-ip-validation.test.ts\nlicense-credits-validation.test.ts\nlicense-key-redirect.test.ts","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"bea079e5-73da-4ef3-846a-01e9a928c9d7","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:43:22.749Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"import () => {\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(5).creditAmount, 5000);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(10).creditAmount, 10000);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(20).creditAmount, 20000);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(37).creditAmount, 37000);\n});\n\ntest(\"credit line item is amount * 100 cents\", () => {\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(5).creditCents, 500);\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(500).creditCents, 50000);\n});\n\ntest(\"processing fee is round(6% of credit cents)\", () => {\n  // 6% of $5 = $0.30 -> 30 cents\n  assert.equal(computeCreditPurchase(5).feeCents, 30);\n  // 6% of $10 = $0.60 -> 60 cents\n=== all test files
